HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_0ecc516c30c2c2a459fd","text":"1. **The worshipper label is placed under the preceding social indictment.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ص ل و/B003` is prescribed worship with its required performance; 107:2's `د ع ع/B001` is harsh exclusion and 107:3's `ح ض ض/B001` is the exhortation that is withheld. **Structural cue:** initial `فـ` carries the warning directly out of the orphan-and-food sequence, while 107:5-107:7 then qualify the worshippers. **Assigned role:** prescribed prayer provides the formal status whose adequacy the surrounding conduct tests. **Abductive move:** the packet does not state a general legal condition for prayer; I infer from the connective sequence that social effects expose whether the prescribed act is being fulfilled in substance. **Reading change:** `للمصلين` is not a protected honorific here; it is a role made answerable to the care it fails to generate.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_3be9db762a6fa32121a3","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"107:4:2\",\"root_000879\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Görünür namaz, itibar kazanmaya yarayan bir tuzağa çevrilebilir; böylece ibadet kişiyi doğrultmak yerine seyircinin beğenisini yakalamaya hizmet eder.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_41d8160d8c3f580a30a7","text":"1. **Woe to practitioners when prayer is severed from mercy.** Evidence: `ص ل و B003` prescribed worship, activated by `ر ح م B001` mercy and `ط ع م B002` feeding another. Structure: prayer enters only after orphan and miskīn failures. Mechanism: the ritual is judged by whether it carries mercy into social action. Abductive move: I infer that the preceding care-failures contextualize “those who pray”; the packet supplies order and branches. Reading change: the woe does not target prayer as such but a praying identity hollowed out by denied care.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_5aa153e66da3ff2ad1ec","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"107:4:2\",\"root_000879\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Uyarı namazın kendisine değil, namaz kimliğini taşıdığı hâlde onu yetim ve yoksula karşı sorumluluğa dönüştürmeyen kişilere yönelir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B005","root_000879/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_790f8ff719e293ab9f3d","text":"4. **A snare of religious form.** Evidence: `ص ل و B004` set snare/trap; activated by `ر ء ي B005` showing off in 107:6 and `م ن ع B006` reciprocal resistance over a thing. Structure: visible prayer, ostentation, and withholding form a trap-like social mechanism. Mechanism: public worship can become a device for catching status while resisting claims of the needy. Abductive move: I supply the trap mechanism; the packet supplies snare, visibility, and resistance branches. Reading change: “for the praying ones” can read as a warning that their own religious display is the trap in which they are caught.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7a7b2d0a2487b03baa47","text":"3. **Prayer can be converted into a snare for public regard.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ص ل و/B004` is a set snare or trap, and 107:6's `ر ء ي/B005` is doing an act so people see it. **Structural cue:** the later relative clause `الذين هم يراءون` specifies the public-facing use of the prayer named here. **Assigned role:** the snare image makes ritual the instrument, with spectators' esteem as the sought catch. **Abductive move:** neither prey nor esteem is named in the packet; I infer them from the trap branch and the explicit desire to be seen. **Reading change:** `المصلين` carries an activated shadow of agents who set prayer as a device for social capture.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_87f269e0568abc69a3ae","text":"2. **Prayer is expected to conduct mercy outward.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ص ل و/B002` includes supplication, blessing, mercy, praise, and purification; 107:0's `ر ح م/B001` supplies beneficent mercy, while 107:3's `ط ع م/B002` and 107:7's `م ن ع/B001` mark feeding and its opposite, withholding. **Structural cue:** mercy opens the window, vulnerable need precedes the prayer label, and blocked benefit closes the sequence. **Assigned role:** prayer is a conduit through which invoked mercy should become blessing for another. **Abductive move:** the packet supplies those endpoints; I infer the transmission arrow from prayer to social beneficence. **Reading change:** the warning falls on prayer that names or seeks mercy but fails to conduct it into food and ordinary aid.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B006"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_8be7f4a5da2935b0a364","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"107:4:2\",\"root_000879\",[\"B006\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Namazın yön veren ilk ilke olmaktan çıkıp itibar arayışının ardından gelen ikinci konuma düşmesi, neden toplumsal davranışı yönetemediğini açıklar.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_9ee7d50f1d09f4e3c8d8","text":"107:5 corrected an overbroad first reaction to the warning: the target is not prescribed prayer as such but prayer held in a relation of heedless distance. 107:6 then activated both the snare (`ص ل و/B004`) and second-place follower (`ص ل و/B006`) images, and 107:7 supplied their social result: ritual captures regard and follows reputation while ordinary benefit remains blocked.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B002"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_c5d207bf46b3041998d1","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"107:4:2\",\"root_000879\",[\"B002\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Dua ve merhamet boyutu taşıyan namaz, başkasına iyilik iletmesi gereken bir kanal hâline gelir; bu iletim kesilince ibadet kendi sahibinin aleyhine tanıklık eder.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B004"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_cff7c079d9c933b4cb9f","text":"107:5-6 narrowed the warning: the woe falls on a praying identity specifically when ṣalāh is detached from attention and redirected toward visibility. 107:7 then made `ص ل و B004` snare more plausible, because visible worship and withheld maʿūn together form a trap of status.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000726/B007","root_000879/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_dcca01772875eb713e67","text":"3. **Fire/heat behind woe.** Evidence: `ص ل و B001` meeting fire and heat; activated by the threat-word ويل and by `س ك ن B007` knife stilling an animal through death. Structure: a root used for prayer also carries fire/roasting hardship. Mechanism: the praying group is linguistically exposed to the root’s punitive heat branch. Abductive move: I infer that woe lets the fire branch resonate; the packet supplies heat/fire and woe context. Reading change: the ayah acquires a latent punitive materiality: the same root that names worship can disclose burning consequence.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_dfba0b913324802ef577","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"107:4:2\",\"root_000879\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Buradaki tehdit, ibadetin adında saklı ateş ve ısı çağrışımını cezalandırıcı, yakıcı bir sonuca dönüştürür.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_000531/B005","root_000879/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_e9f5890be996d157b545","text":"4. **The race-follower image exposes a reversal of priority.** **Lexical evidence:** fixed `ص ل و/B006` is the second horse following at the leader's rear flank; 107:6's `ر ء ي/B005` supplies showmanship before people. **Structural cue:** the worshipper noun is followed by clauses that reveal heedlessness, audience-seeking, and withheld aid. **Assigned role:** the second-place branch models prayer as following another leader rather than directing conduct. **Abductive move:** this is exploratory because the equine image belongs to a distinct nominal use; I infer from 107:6 that public approval leads and ritual trails behind it. **Reading change:** the worshippers can be heard as followers whose prayer occupies second place to reputation, explaining why it does not govern their treatment of others.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000879/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_ee1ec6adb8896300a9f1","text":"2. **Prayer as supplication/mercy inverted.** Evidence: `ص ل و B002` supplication, praise, mercy; activated by the double `ر ح م` in 107:0 and non-urging for food in 107:3. Structure: the prayer-root shares mercy language with the opening but is surrounded by merciless conduct. Mechanism: a practice whose branch can be mercy becomes evidence against its doer when it fails to generate mercy. Abductive move: I supply the evaluative inversion; the packet supplies mercy in both root inventories. Reading change: المصلين can mean people near a mercy-channel who have blocked its output.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
